# Runtime visual lint

Generated by `tools/visual_qa/lint_report.py` from `a`. A report, not a gate (it needs a renderer).

Rules: **font** (a: < 12 x text scale, or a size override off the §4.2 scale), **overlap** (b), **clipped** (c), **contrast** (d: < 4.5:1, 3:1 at >= 24 px, background sampled from the PNG). (e) Text drawn by a script's own `_draw` (`draw_string`) is invisible to the tree walk; each screen lists those scripts under *custom-drawn*. Overlap counts pairs; a modal over a page also counts (the walk can't see occlusion). Contrast marked *low confidence* had a glyph pixel that does reach the minimum (the declared colour may not be what is drawn).
